# Central Dublin: a place on the globe (3D Tiles 1.1)

Made by [cwplans/tools/build-place-tiles.mjs](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/tools/build-place-tiles.mjs) for the region config [docklands/geo/regions/dublin.js](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/docklands/geo/regions/dublin.js); drawn by the globe of the Three.js port ([docklands/globe/places.js](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/docklands/globe/places.js)).
This README is generated from `provenance/log.json` and [cwplans/data-register.json](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/data-register.json) by `node cwplans/tools/build-place-tiles.mjs readme --region dublin` (also at the end of each build). Do not edit it by hand.

- Files served from https://danbri.github.io/docklandia/data/ie/dublin/ (repository https://github.com/danbri/docklandia, folder `data/ie/dublin/`); the index entry is in londat's [cwplans/docklands/data/places/index.json](https://github.com/danbri/londat/blob/main/cwplans/docklands/data/places/index.json)

## What is derived from what

- `place-select-osm`: OSM elements -> buildings and parts (outlines, height, levels and roof tags, names), areas, lines, named points.
- `place-heights`: the selection + national building parts (if any; raw only) + raster height crops in `sources/` -> one height, height source and roof per building (fault F55 counts).
- `place-tiles`: the selection + heights + the DEM grid + the EGM96 geoid grid (`docklands/data/world/geoid-egm96-1deg.png`) + the DEM ring grid -> `tiles/*.glb`, `tileset.json`, `features.json`.
- `place-select-outer`: the Overture answers (roads, streets, water, places, peaks, city buildings) -> roads, rail, water, place names, peaks; the city buildings whose geometry is from OpenStreetMap; the height values of the other records (open licence), and the IGN floors apart.
- `place-city-heights`: the city buildings + the national parts + the raster crop + the IGN floors (switch) + Overture and Microsoft heights -> one height per city building (the place-heights order, no roofs).
- `place-lod`: the outer selection + city heights + the surroundings DEM + the land cover + the geoid + the core tileset -> the tiles of the city and the surroundings, `tileset.json` (the whole tree), `surround.json`.
- `index.json` (one level up) and this README are made from these files.

## City buildings by geometry class

OSM first: an IGN or Microsoft footprint that overlaps an OSM outline is dropped. Each extra class has its own register source and its own switch: its adapter id in `place.surround.geometry` of the region config (delete the id, rebuild). The block card names the class.

| class | footprint source | register source | switch | buildings drawn | dropped (overlap OSM) | blocks in tiles | heights by source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| osm | OpenStreetMap (via Overture) | `osm` | (always) | 491393 | 0 | 410179 | ghsl 184845, osm-levels 176828, default-small 54859, overture-height 45640, ms 26759, default 1790, osm-height 672 |
| ms | Microsoft ML Buildings (via Overture) | `overture-ms-geometry` | `overture-ms-geometry` | 25247 | 43 | 7519 | overture-height 24766, ghsl 467, default 14 |

Blocks in tiles count a building once per level that draws it (levels 13 and 14, and 15 next to the core); buildings under 40 m2 are not drawn as blocks.

## Not committed

Raw downloads stay in `cwplans/data/raw/places/dublin/` (in cwplans/.gitignore): `data/raw/places/dublin/osm.json.gz` (osm), `data/raw/places/dublin/dem.json.gz` (dem), `data/raw/places/dublin/dem-surround.bin.gz` (dem-surround), `data/raw/places/dublin/landcover.bin.gz` (landcover), `data/raw/places/dublin/ms-city.json.gz` (heights-ms-city), `data/raw/places/dublin/overture-roads-2026-09-23.1.json` (overture-roads), `data/raw/places/dublin/overture-streets-2026-09-23.1.json` (overture-streets), `data/raw/places/dublin/overture-water-2026-09-23.1.json` (overture-water), `data/raw/places/dublin/overture-places-2026-09-23.1.json` (overture-places), `data/raw/places/dublin/overture-peaks-2026-09-23.1.json` (overture-peaks), `data/raw/places/dublin/overture-buildings-2026-09-23.1.json` (overture-buildings), the original zips and GeoTIFFs, and the intermediates in `work/`. Run `node cwplans/tools/build-place-tiles.mjs fetch --region dublin` to get them again (the hashes above say whether the source changed).

## Review status

- OSM (ODbL 1.0, share-alike): allowed for the prototyping phase (owner, 2026-10-03); to be reviewed with the rest of the OSM use.
- world-overture: licence class "share-alike" (ODbL 1.0 (buildings theme; per record: the licence of each source in its sources list)); allowed by the owner on 2026-10-20 ("Use ms/overture"), registered next to OSM. Values whose own source licence is not open (for example an empty licence field) are neither used nor committed: see the counts in sources/.
- world-ms-buildings: licence class "share-alike" (ODbL 1.0); allowed by the owner on 2026-10-20 ("Use ms/overture"), registered next to OSM. Values whose own source licence is not open (for example an empty licence field) are neither used nor committed: see the counts in sources/.
- world-overture-outer: licence class "share-alike" (ODbL 1.0 (OpenStreetMap-derived records; the record sources list names each source and licence)): OpenStreetMap data as redistributed by Overture; only records whose source is OpenStreetMap are drawn (geometry_sources), counted in log.json outer_counts.
- Copernicus DEM GLO-30: its full credit ends "all rights reserved" (CLAUDE.md); accepted by the owner on 2026-10-20 ("Data - is fine for now"); re-check before the prototyping phase ends.
